'../types';\n\nconst CubeCanvas = ({ config, onManualRotateStart }: CubeCanvasProps) => {\n  const containerRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);\n  const sceneRef = useRef<THREE.Scene | null>(null);\n  const cameraRef = useRef<THREE.PerspectiveCamera | null>(null);\n  const rendererRef = useRef<THREE.WebGLRenderer | null>(null);\n  const cubeRef = useRef<THREE.Mesh | null>(null);\n  const edgesRef = useRef<THREE.LineSegments | null>(null);\n  const configRef = useRef(config);\n\n  // ドラッグ操作用のRef\n  const isDraggingRef = useRef(false);\n  const previousPointerPositionRef = useRef({ x: 0, y: 0 });\n\n  // 最新のconfigを常に参照できるように更新\n  useEffect(() => {\n    configRef.current = config;\n  }, [config]);\n\n  // マテリアルの色とワイヤーフレームの動的反映\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (cubeRef.current) {\n      const material = cubeRef.current.material as THREE.MeshStandardMaterial;\n      material.color.set(config.color);\n      material.wireframe = config.wireframe;\n      material.needsUpdate = true;\n    }\n  }, [config.color, config.wireframe]);\n\n  // ズーム（カメラ距離）の動的反映\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (cameraRef.current) {\n      cameraRef.current.position.z = config.zoom;\n    }\n  }, [config.zoom]);\n\n  // Three.js 初期化 & アニメーションループ\n  useEffect(() => {\n    const container = containerRef.current;\n    if (!container) return;\n\n    const width = container.clientWidth;\n    const height = container.clientHeight;\n\n    // シーン\n    const scene = new THREE.Scene();\n    sceneRef.current = scene;\n\n    // カメラ\n    const camera = new THREE.PerspectiveCamera(50, width / height, 0.1, 1000);\n    camera.position.z = configRef.current.zoom;\n    cameraRef.current = camera;\n\n    // レンダラー\n    const renderer = new THREE.WebGLRenderer({ antialias: true, alpha: true });\n    renderer.setSize(width, height);\n    renderer.setPixelRatio(Math.min(window.devicePixelRatio, 2));\n    renderer.toneMapping = THREE.ACESFilmicToneMapping;\n    renderer.toneMappingExposure = 1.0;\n    rendererRef.current = renderer;\n\n    container.appendChild(renderer.domElement);\n\n    // 立方体ジオメトリ & マテリアル\n    const geometry = new THREE.BoxGeometry(1.4, 1.4, 1.4);\n    const material = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({\n      color: configRef.current.color,\n      wireframe: configRef.current.wireframe,\n      roughness: 0.3,\n      metalness: 0.2,\n    });\n    const cube = new THREE.Mesh(geometry, material);\n    cubeRef.current = cube;\n    scene.add(cube);\n\n    // キューブの輪郭エッジ線\n    const edgesGeometry = new THREE.EdgesGeometry(geometry);\n    const edgesMaterial = new THREE.LineBasicMaterial({\n      color: 0xffffff,\n      transparent: true,\n      opacity: 0.35,\n    });\n    const edges = new THREE.LineSegments(edgesGeometry, edgesMaterial);\n    edgesRef.current = edges;\n    cube.add(edges);\n\n    // ライト設定\n    const ambientLight = new THREE.AmbientLight(0xffffff, 0.8);\n    scene.add(ambientLight);\n\n    const dirLight1 = new THREE.DirectionalLight(0xffffff, 1.2);\n    dirLight1.position.set(5, 5, 5);\n    scene.add(dirLight1);\n\n    const dirLight2 = new THREE.DirectionalLight(0x38bdf8, 0.6);\n    dirLight2.position.set(-5, -3, -5);\n    scene.add(dirLight2);\n\n    // アニメーションループ\n    let animationFrameId: number;\n    const animate = () => {\n      animationFrameId = cubeRef.current.rotation.y += speed;\n        cubeRef.current.rotation.x += speed * 0.5;\n      }\n\n      renderer.render(scene, camera);\n    };\n    animate();\n\n    // リサイズ監視\n    const newHeight > 0 && cameraRef.current && rendererRef.current) {\n          cameraRef.current.aspect = newWidth / newHeight;\n          cameraRef.current.updateProjectionMatrix();\n          rendererRef.current.setSize(newWidth, newHeight);\n        }\n      }\n    });\n    resizeObserver.observe(container);\n\n    // クリーンアップ\n    return () => {\n      cancelAnimationFrame(animationFrameId);\n      resizeObserver.disconnect();\n      if (container.contains(renderer.domElement)) {\n        container.removeChild(renderer.domElement);\n      }\n      geometry.dispose();\n      material.dispose();\n      edgesGeometry.dispose();\n      edgesMaterial.dispose();\n      renderer.dispose();\n    };\n  }, []);\n\n  // ポインター操作（マウスドラッグ・タッチ対応）\n  const (e.target as HTMLElement).setPointerCapture(e.pointerId);\n    onManualRotateStart?.();\n  };\n\n  const handlePointerMove = (e: React.PointerEvent<HTMLDivElement>) => {\n    if (!isDraggingRef.current || !cubeRef.current) return;\n\n    const deltaX = e.clientX - previousPointerPositionRef.current.x;\n    const deltaY = e.clientY - previousPointerPositionRef.current.y;\n\n e.clientX,\n      y: e.clientY,\n    };\n  };\n\n  const handlePointerUp = (e: React.PointerEvent<HTMLDivElement>) => {\n    isDraggingRef.current = false;\n    try {\n      (e.target as HTMLElement).releasePointerCapture(e.pointerId);\n    } catch {\n      // ポインターキャプチャ未保持時の安全策\n    }\n  };\n\n  return (\n    <div\n      className=\"canvas-container\"\n      ref={containerRef}\n      onPointerDown={handlePointerDown}\n      onPointerMove={handlePointerMove}\n      onPointerUp={handlePointerUp}\n      onPointerCancel={handlePointerUp}\n    >\n      <div className=\"hint-badge\">\n configRef = useRef(config);\n\n  // ドラッグ操作用のRef\n  const isDraggingRef = useRef(false);\n  const previousPointerPositionRef = useRef({ x: 0, y: 0 });\n\n  // 最新のconfigを常に参照できるように更新\n  useEffect(() => {\n    configRef.current = config;\n  }, [config]);\n\n  // マテリアルの色とワイヤーフレームの動的反映\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (cubeRef.current) {\n      const material = cubeRef.current.material as THREE.MeshStandardMaterial;\n      material.color.set(config.color);\n      material.wireframe = config.wireframe;\n      material.needsUpdate = true;\n    }\n  }, [config.color, config.wireframe]);\n\n  // ズーム（カメラ距離）の動的反映\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (cameraRef.current) {\n      cameraRef.current.position.z = config.zoom;\n    }\n  }, [config.zoom]);\n\n  // Three.js 初期化 & アニメーションループ\n  useEffect(() => {\n    const container = containerRef.current;\n    if (!container) return;\n\n    const width = container.clientWidth || 300;\n    const height = container.clientHeight || 300;\n\n    // シーン\n    const scene = new THREE.Scene();\n    sceneRef.current = scene;\n\n    // カメラ\n    const camera = new THREE.PerspectiveCamera(50, width / height, 0.1, 1000);\n    camera.position.z = configRef.current.zoom;\n    cameraRef.current = camera;\n\n    // レンダラー\n    const renderer = new THREE.WebGLRenderer({ antialias: true, alpha: true, powerPreference: 'high-performance' });\n    renderer.setSize(width, height);\n    renderer.setPixelRatio(Math.min(window.devicePixelRatio, 2));\n    renderer.toneMapping = THREE.ACESFilmicToneMapping;\n    renderer.toneMappingExposure = 1.0;\n    rendererRef.current = renderer;\n\n    container.appendChild(renderer.domElement);\n\n    // 立方体ジオメトリ & マテリアル\n    const geometry = new THREE.BoxGeometry(1.4, 1.4, 1.4);\n    const material = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({\n      color: configRef.current.color,\n      wireframe: configRef.current.wireframe,\n      roughness: 0.3,\n      metalness: 0.2,\n    });\n    const cube = new THREE.Mesh(geometry, material);\n    cubeRef.current = cube;\n    scene.add(cube);\n\n    // キューブの輪郭エッジ線\n    const edgesGeometry = new THREE.EdgesGeometry(geometry);\n    const edgesMaterial = new THREE.LineBasicMaterial({\n      color: 0xffffff,\n      transparent: true,\n      opacity: 0.35,\n    });\n    const edges = new THREE.LineSegments(edgesGeometry, edgesMaterial);\n    edgesRef.current = edges;\n    cube.add(edges);\n\n    // ライト設定\n    const ambientLight = new THREE.AmbientLight(0xffffff, 0.8);\n    scene.add(ambientLight);\n\n    const dirLight1 = new THREE.DirectionalLight(0xffffff, 1.2);\n    dirLight1.position.set(5, 5, 5);\n    scene.add(dirLight1);\n\n    const dirLight2 = new THREE.DirectionalLight(0x38bdf8, 0.6);\n    dirLight2.position.set(-5, -3, -5);\n    scene.add(dirLight2);\n\n    // アニメーションループ\n    let animationFrameId: number;\n    const animate = () => {\n      animationFrameId = requestAnimationFrame(animate);\n\n      if (cubeRef.current && configRef.current.autoRotate && !isDraggingRef.current) {\n        const speed = configRef.current.rotateSpeed * 0.01;\n        cubeRef.current.rotation.y += speed;\n        cubeRef.current.rotation.x += speed * 0.5;\n      }\n\n      renderer.render(scene, camera);\n    };\n    animate();\n\n    // リサイズ監視\n    const resizeObserver = new ResizeObserver((entries) => {\n      for (const entry of entries) {\n        const { width: newWidth, height: newHeight } = entry.contentRect;\n        if (newWidth > 0 && newHeight > 0 && cameraRef.current && rendererRef.current) {\n          cameraRef.current.aspect = newWidth / newHeight;\n          cameraRef.current.updateProjectionMatrix();\n          rendererRef.current.setSize(newWidth, newHeight);\n        }\n      }\n    });\n    resizeObserver.observe(container);\n\n    // ネイティブホイールイベント（preventDefaultでページスクロール抑制）\n    const handleNativeWheel = (e: WheelEvent) => {\n      e.preventDefault();\n      if (!cameraRef.current) return;\n      const zoomStep = e.deltaY * 0.0025;\n      const minZoom = 1.8;\n      const maxZoom = 5.5;\n      const nextZoom = Math.min(Math.max(cameraRef.current.position.z + zoomStep, minZoom), maxZoom);\n      cameraRef.current.position.z = nextZoom;\n    };\n    container.addEventListener('wheel', handleNativeWheel, { passive: false });\n\n    // WebGLコンテキスト喪失ハンドラー\n    const handleContextLost = (e: Event) => {\n      e.preventDefault();\n      cancelAnimationFrame(animationFrameId);\n    };\n    renderer.domElement.addEventListener('webglcontextlost', handleContextLost, false);\n\n    // クリーンアップ\n    return () => {\n      cancelAnimationFrame(animationFrameId);\n      container.removeEventListener('wheel', handleNativeWheel);\n      renderer.domElement.removeEventListener('webglcontextlost', handleContextLost);\n      resizeObserver.disconnect();\n      if (container.contains(renderer.domElement)) {\n        container.removeChild(renderer.domElement);\n      }\n      geometry.dispose();\n      material.dispose();\n      edgesGeometry.dispose();\n      edgesMaterial.dispose();\n      renderer.dispose();\n    };\n  }, []);\n\n  // ポインター操作（マウスドラッグ・タッチ対応）\n  const handlePointerDown = (e: React.PointerEvent<HTMLDivElement>) => {\n    isDraggingRef.current = true;\n    previousPointerPositionRef.current = {\n      x: e.clientX,\n      y: e.clientY,\n    };\n    (e.target as HTMLElement).setPointerCapture(e.pointerId);\n    onManualRotateStart?.();\n  };\n\n  const handlePointerMove = (e: React.PointerEvent<HTMLDivElement>) => {\n    if (!isDraggingRef.current || !cubeRef.current || !containerRef.current) return;\n\n    const deltaX = e.clientX - previousPointerPositionRef.current.x;\n    const deltaY = e.clientY - previousPointerPositionRef.current.y;\n\n    // 画面サイズと回転速度設定に応じた動的な感度計算\n    const rect = containerRef.current.getBoundingClientRect();\n    const minDim = Math.min(rect.width, rect.height) || 400;\n    const baseFactor = 3.6 / minDim;\n    const speedRatio = Math.max(0.6, Math.min(configRef.current.rotateSpeed, 2.5));\n    const rotateFactor = baseFactor * speedRatio;\n\n    cubeRef.current.rotation.y += deltaX * rotateFactor;\n    cubeRef.current.rotation.x += deltaY * rotateFactor;\n\n    previousPointerPositionRef.current = {\n      x: e.clientX,\n      y: e.clientY,\n    };\n  };\n\n  const handlePointerEnd = useCallback((e: React.PointerEvent<HTMLDivElement>) => {\n    if (!isDraggingRef.current) return;\n    isDraggingRef.current = false;\n    try {\n      (e.target as HTMLElement).releasePointerCapture(e.pointerId);\n    } catch {\n      // ポインターキャプチャ未保持時の安全策\n    }\n    onManualRotateEnd?.();\n  }, [onManualRotateEnd]);\n\n  return (\n    <div\n      className=\"canvas-container\"\n      ref={containerRef}\n      onPointerDown={handlePointerDown}\n      onPointerMove={handlePointerMove}\n      onPointerUp={handlePointerEnd}\n      onPointerCancel={handlePointerEnd}\n    >\n      <div className=\"hint-badge\">\n        <span>👆 ドラッグで360度回転 / ホイールでズーム</span>\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n};\n\nexport default CubeCanvas;","language":"tsx"}

'./components/CubeCanvas';\nimport ControlPanel from './components/ControlPanel';\n\nconst App = () => {\n  const [config, setConfig] = useState<CubeConfig>(INITIAL_CONFIG);\n  const ...prev,\n        ...newConfig,\n      };\n      if (newConfig.autoRotate !== undefined) {\n        wasAutoRotatingRef.current = newConfig.autoRotate;\n      }\n      return updated;\n    });\n  },       };\n      }\n      return prev;\n    });\n  }, []);\n\n  const handleManualRotateEnd = useCallback(() => {\n    if (wasAutoRotatingRef.current) {\n      setConfig((prev) => ({\n        ...prev,\n        autoRotate: true,\n      }));\n    }\n  }, []);\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"app-container\">\n      <header className=\"header\">\n        <h1   };\n  }, []);\n\n  // ポインター操作（マウスドラッグ・タッチ対応）\n  const handlePointerDown = (e: React.PointerEvent<HTMLDivElement>) => {\n    isDraggingRef.current = true;\n    previousPointerPositionRef.current = baseFactor * speedRatio;\n\n    cubeRef.current.rotation.y += deltaX * rotateFactor;\n    cubeRef.current.rotation.x += deltaY * rotateFactor;\n\n    previousPointerPositionRef.current = {\n      x: // カメラ\n    const camera = new THREE.PerspectiveCamera(50, width / height, 0.1, 1000);\n    camera.position.z = configRef.current.zoom;\n    cameraRef.current = camera;\n\n    // レンダラー\n    const cubeRef.current = cube;\n    scene.add(cube);\n\n    // キューブの輪郭エッジ線\n    const edgesGeometry = new THREE.EdgesGeometry(geometry);\n    const edgesMaterial = new THREE.LineBasicMaterial({\n      color: 0xffffff,\n      transparent: true,\n      opacity: 0.35,\n    });\n    const edges = new THREE.LineSegments(edgesGeometry, edgesMaterial);\n    edgesRef.current = edges;\n    cube.add(edges);\n\n    // ライト設定\n    const ambientLight = new THREE.AmbientLight(0xffffff, 0.8);\n    scene.add(ambientLight);\n\n    const dirLight1 = new THREE.DirectionalLight(0xffffff, 1.2);\n    dirLight1.position.set(5, 5, 5);\n    scene.add(dirLight1);\n\n    const dirLight2 = new THREE.DirectionalLight(0x38bdf8, 0.6);\n    dirLight2.position.set(-5, -3, -5);\n    scene.add(dirLight2);\n\n newHeight;\n          cameraRef.current.updateProjectionMatrix();\n          rendererRef.current.setSize(newWidth, newHeight);\n        }\n      }\n    });\n    resizeObserver.observe(container);\n\n    // ネイティブホイールイベント（preventDefaultでページスクロール抑制 & 親状態と同期）\n    const handleNativeWheel = (e: WheelEvent) => {\n      e.preventDefault();\n      if (!cameraRef.current) return;\n      const zoomStep = cameraRef.current.position.z = nextZoom;\n      onZoomChangeRef.current?.(Number(nextZoom.toFixed(1)));\n    };\n    container.addEventListener('wheel', handleNativeWheel, { passive: false });\n\n    // WebGLコンテキスト喪失ハンドラー\n    const handleContextLost = (e: Event) => {\n      e.preventDefault();\n      cancelAnimationFrame(animationFrameId);\n    };\n    renderer.domElement.addEventListener('webglcontextlost', handleContextLost, false);\n\n    // クリーンアップ\n    return () => {\n      cancelAnimationFrame(animationFrameId);\n      container.removeEventListener('wheel', handleNativeWheel);\n      renderer.domElement.removeEventListener('webglcontextlost', handleContextLost);\n      resizeObserver.disconnect();\n      if (container.contains(renderer.domElement)) {\n        container.removeChild(renderer.domElement);\n      }\n      geometry.dispose();\n      material.dispose();\n      edgesGeometry.dispose();\n      edgesMaterial.dispose();\n      renderer.dispose();\n    };\n  }, []);\n\n  // ポインター操作（マウスドラッグ・タッチ対応）\n  const handlePointerDown = (e: React.PointerEvent<HTMLDivElement>) => {\n    isDraggingRef.current = true;\n    previousPointerPositionRef.current = {\n      x: e.clientX,\n      y: e.clientY,\n    };\n    (e.target as HTMLElement).setPointerCapture(e.pointerId);\n    onManualRotateStart?.();\n  };\n\n  const handlePointerMove = (e: React.PointerEvent<HTMLDivElement>) => {\n    if (!isDraggingRef.current || !cubeRef.current || !containerRef.current) return;\n\n    const deltaX = e.clientX - previousPointerPositionRef.current.x;\n    const deltaY = e.clientY - previousPointerPositionRef.current.y;\n\n    // 画面サイズと回転速度設定に応じた動的な感度計算\n    const rect = containerRef.current.getBoundingClientRect();\n    const minDim = Math.min(rect.width, rect.height) || 400;\n    const baseFactor = 3.6 / minDim;\n    const speedRatio = Math.max(0.6, Math.min(configRef.current.rotateSpeed, 2.5));\n    const rotateFactor = baseFactor * speedRatio;\n\n    cubeRef.current.rotation.y += deltaX * rotateFactor;\n    cubeRef.current.rotation.x += deltaY
